- **spatial-data-architect**: 空間與地理資料分析架構師(區域研究 / hedonic 房價 / 不動產實證)。把帶有地址或經緯度的資料變成可分析、可畫圖、可發表的空間研究資料集。核心能力:(1)LLM 輔助地理編碼＋驗證退路——地址轉座標後,用行政區界/中心點/白名單比對抓出離群與幻覺,絕不讓 AI 亂猜的座標進資料;(2)H3 六角網格空間聚合——面積相等、鄰居一致、解析度可調,比行政區更適合密度與比較,且天然去識別;(3)座標系統紀律——台灣官方 TWD97(EPSG:3826,公尺)與 WGS84(EPSG:4326,經緯度)不可混用,附轉換與誤用防線;(4)空間計量意識——空間自相關(Moran's I)、空間落後/誤差模型的診斷,估計交棒 r-spss;(5)出版級空間地圖——區域面量圖 choropleth、六角密度圖 hexbin 的出圖配方(靜態、可放論文與簡報),與 management-figure 的統計圖劃分。附已實測的 H3 聚合腳本。何時用:全台不動產/房價的區域分析、把成交點聚合成網格、地址轉座標要驗證、公司地理定位、要畫區域地圖、hedonic 定價模型的空間資料準備、鄰近效果/空間外溢。觸發詞:空間分析、地理資料、GIS、地理編碼、geocoding、經緯度、座標轉換、TWD97、WGS84、EPSG、H3、六角網格、hexbin、choropleth、面量圖、區域地圖、空間… ([Nero1688/claude-academic-skills](https://github.com/Nero1688/claude-academic-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-data-architect) · ★13 · 3 copies)

- **spatial-experience-design**: Use when inventing, comparing, shaping, extending, or critiquing the visual-spatial experience of an interior, architecture, furniture, decor, gallery, showroom, staging, or other spatial website. - **spatial-expression-map-visualization**: Use when you need to render gene expression as 2D spatial maps keyed to spot/cell coordinates — e.g. visually validating CeLEry-predicted 2D coordinates against known spatial gradients (ERBB2, ESR1, GATA3) or inspecting VAE-generated ST replicates against originals. Use it when asked to "join a table to a layer", "spatial join points to polygons", "match records by ID", "why didn't my join match", "low match rate", or when a join returns too many rows. - **spatial-slice-alignment-3d**: Use when multiple adjacent tissue slices profiled by seq-based ST (e.g., 10x Visium) must be combined into a single 3D coordinate system before cell type identification or spatially informed modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-slice-alignment-3d))
- **spatial-slice-quality-qc**: Quality-control ordered spatial-transcriptomics slices before alignment, using bilateral tissue-loss and capture-loss evidence, multi-domain review, display-only rigid preregistration, and traceable retain/exclude outputs. Use with the companion spatial-slice-quality-viewer for the current multi-panel report. ([BrainStOrmics/Spateo-Skills](https://github.com/BrainStOrmics/Spateo-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-slice-quality-qc) · ★1)
- **spatial-slice-quality-viewer**: Generate the current Spateo Referee retain/exclude report with overview, statistics, multi-metric and bilateral-loss evidence, all slices, methods, and shared-frame ROI. Supports one QC run or a dataset collection using the companion QC skill's canonical renderer. ([BrainStOrmics/Spateo-Skills](https://github.com/BrainStOrmics/Spateo-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-slice-quality-qc/subskills/spatial-slice-quality-viewer) · ★1)

- **spatial-spectral-array-processing**: Use when you have preprocessed MSImagingArrays data (after normalization, smoothing, and baseline reduction) and need to detect peaks consistently across all spectra in an imaging experiment, create reference peak positions from a representative subset, and filter by signal-to-noise ratio and. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spatial-spectral-array-processing) · 2 copies)
- **spatial-spot-coordinate-registration**: Use when when you have paired spatial transcriptome and metabolome datasets with spot-based coordinates that need to be aligned for multi-modal integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spatial-spot-coordinate-registration) · 2 copies)
- **spatial-spot-quality-filtering**: Use when a spatially barcoded ST dataset (e.g., MOB ST from SRA SRR3382373, ~260–262 spots) is being prepared for poly(A) site identification with scAPAtrap and downstream stAPAminer APA usage quantification, and low-count spots must be excluded. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spatial-spot-quality-filtering))
- **spatial-sql**: Write, review, debug or optimize spatial SQL on an already chosen engine, including DuckDB Spatial, PostGIS, BigQuery GIS, Snowflake and Sedona. Use for spatial predicates, joins, indexes, query plans, CRS and metric/geodesic correctness. Route unresolved engine or cross-system architecture choices to open-map-stack; ordinary non-spatial SQL and casual place questions are outside this skill. ([jaakla/openmapstack-skills](https://github.com/jaakla/openmapstack-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-sql) · ★78 · 2 copies)
- **spatial-sql**: Writes and checks spatial SQL in PostGIS and DuckDB spatial. Covers SRID and ST_Transform before measuring, geometry vs geography, spatial indexes (GiST, R-tree) and the && operator, ST_DWithin instead of ST_Distance in WHERE, ST_Intersects vs ST_Contains vs ST_Within, nearest neighbor with &lt;-&gt;, ST_MakeValid, ST_Subdivide, clustering (ST_ClusterDBSCAN, ST_ClusterKMeans), DuckDB ST_Read, GeoParquet and bbox filters on Overture data, axis order in DuckDB, loading data with ogr2ogr or shp2pgsql, and checking a query with EXPLAIN and counts. Use when the user writes SQL with ST_ functions, asks a… ([TerraLabAI/geoai-skills](https://github.com/TerraLabAI/geoai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-sql) · ★2)
- **spatial-sql**: Write correct, fast, read-only spatial SQL for PostGIS, Oracle Spatial (SDO_RELATE, SDO_WITHIN_DISTANCE) and SQL Server (STIntersects, geography), covering intersects, within a distance, nearest, bounding-box filters, areas and lengths. Use when asked to query a spatial database, find features inside or near something, write or fix a spatial SQL query, or when a query is slow, gives distances in the wrong units, or is refused by sql_select. ([wiatrek-kevin-s/columbus](https://github.com/wiatrek-kevin-s/columbus/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-sql) · ★2)
- **spatial-ssrl-spatial-understanding-rl**: Improve spatial reasoning in vision-language models through five automatically-formulated pretext tasks (patch shuffling, flipping, inpainting, depth ordering, 3D position) that require zero human annotation, using only RGB/RGB-D images and RL optimization for 4% accuracy gains. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/spatial-ssrl-spatial-understanding-rl) · ★7)
- **spatial-statistics**: --&gt; ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-transcriptomics-analysis/bioSkills/spatial-statistics) · ★3.1k · 15 copies)
- **spatial-statistics**: Load when running spatial autocorrelation / hotspot / co-occurrence / neighbourhood-enrichment / Ripley K stats on a clustered spatial AnnData via squidpy. Skip when ranking spatially variable genes (use spatial-genes); tissue domain detection (use spatial-domains). ([TianGzlab/OmicsClaw](https://github.com/TianGzlab/OmicsClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial/spatial-statistics) · ★161 · 2 copies)
- **spatial-statistics**: Always invoke before testing a geographic pattern for clustering, hotspots, dependence, or explanatory regression, even when aggregation or ordinary OLS is proposed as routine. Covers Moran's I, LISA, Getis-Ord Gi*, weights, MAUP and scale sensitivity for areas/grids, residual dependence, and spatial lag/error/GWR/MGWR models. Use ML standards for predictive evaluation and geostatistics for continuous surfaces from sparse samples. ([muend/geoai-skills](https://github.com/muend/geoai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spatial-statistics) · ★46 · 5 copies)
- **spatial-statistics**: Load when running spatial autocorrelation / hotspot / co-occurrence / neighbourhood-enrichment / Ripley K stats on a clustered spatial AnnData via squidpy. Skip when ranking spatially variable genes (use spatial-genes) or for tissue domain detection (use spatial-domains). - **spatial-statistics**: Spatial statistics for spatial transcriptomics using neighborhood enrichment, Ripley's statistics, co-occurrence, Moran/Geary autocorrelation, local Moran, Getis-Ord Gi*, bivariate Moran, and spatial graph centrality summaries.
